import numpy as np, time, math
import scipy.optimize as opt maybe

def normalize(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, float)
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return a*(target/s)

def fft_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0: return inf
    # maybe normalize to sum 1 to avoid big numbers? Score invariant, but for FFT stability do p=a/S.
    p=a/S
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*A.conjugate(), N)
    # due numerical, C[n?..]
    maxc = float(np.max(C[:2*n-1]))
    if maxc <0: maxc=0
    return 2.0*n*maxc  # since sum=1
```

Let's design exact/fast score correctness:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12: return inf
    p=a/s
    out_len=2*n-1
    N=1
    # Use next fast length power of two
    while N < out_len:
        N <<=1
    # For very large n (e.g., 100k), N maybe 262144; ok. But if n huge memory? We limit n.
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    spec=F.real*F.real + F.imag*F.imag
    c=np.fft.irfft(spec, N)
    # Because of circular convolution zero padding, first out_len correct.
    maxc=float(np.max(c[:out_len]))
    if maxc < 0 or not np.isfinite(maxc): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*maxc)  # s=1
```
Wait if a not normalized, scaling invariant; using p sum1 gives objective exactly. Good. For exact evaluator, it clips >1000; our p sum1 no issue.

Potential FFT numerical: For large n and small p, spec values ~n*(1/N?)? p ~1/n, FFT magnitude sum~1, spec~1, irfft~1/n. Fine.

`_make_sym_power`:
```
def _make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift=0.5):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left=i+shift
    right=n - i + (shift - 1.0)  # for shift=0.5: n-i-0.5
    # ensure positive
    left = np.maximum(left, 1e-12)
    right = np.maximum(right, 1e-12)
    if alpha == 0:
       return np.ones(n)
    return np.power(left, -alpha) * np.power(right, -alpha)
```
For shift=1.0: right=n-i, left=i+1. Good. For shift=0.25: right=n-i-0.25.

left = i+shift

Golden search details:
```
def _golden_minimize(func, a, b, tol=1e-3, max_iter=40):
    phi=(5**0.5-1)/2
    c=b-phi*(b-a); d=a+phi*(b-a); fc=func(c); fd=func(d)
    for ...:
       if fc < fd: b=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=b-phi*(b-a); fc=func(c)
       else: a=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=a+phi*(b-a); fd=func(d)
    return 0.5*(a+b)
```

def _make_perturbed_sym_power(n, alpha, shift, coeff, modes=(1,2,3,4,6,8)):
    base=_make_sym_power(...)
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    mod=base.copy()

    return mod
```

Implementation of `_fast_score` should be robust for empty, nonfinite. It clips to 1000? Our sequences small; okay. It uses `np.isfinite(x).all()` maybe.

```
def _fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n ==0 or not np.isfinite(a).all(): return float('inf')
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(s): return inf
    p=a/s
    out=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N < out:
       N *=2
    # If N too large? If N>1<<20, maybe use direct? But n limited.
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    spec=F.real*F.real + F.imag*F.imag
    c=np.fft.irfft(spec, N)
    # Due roundoff, use slice
    maxc=float(np.max(c[:out]))
    if not math.isfinite(maxc) or maxc < 0.0:
       if maxc < 0.0: maxc=0.0
       else return inf
    return float(2.0*n*maxc)
```
If maxc tiny negative due all zero? p positive, maxc positive. Good.

Golden implementation:
```
def _golden_alpha(n, shift, lo, hi, max_iter=30):
    ph=0.6180339887498949
    c=hi - ph*(hi-lo); d=lo + ph*(hi-lo)
    fc=_alpha_score(c,n,shift); fd=...
    for _ in range(max_iter):
       if fc < fd:
          hi=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=hi-ph*(hi-lo); fc=_alpha_score(c,n,shift)
       else:
          lo=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=lo+ph*(hi-lo); fd=_alpha_score(d,n,shift)
       if abs(hi-lo)<1e-4: break
    return 0.5*(lo+hi)
```

def _insert_top(top, score, arr, limit=6):

      top.append((score, arr.copy()))

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """..."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    candidates=[]
    # parent
    try:
       parent = kwargs.get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if parent is not None:
          p = normalize_sum(np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64),1.0)
          if p.size>0 and np.isfinite(p).all() and p.sum()>1e-6:
             sc = fast_score(p)
             if np.isfinite(sc): candidates.append((sc,p.copy()))
    except Exception: pass
    # uniform fallback
    u=np.ones(256,dtype=np.float64)
    candidates.append((fast_score(u), u.copy()))
    # exploration
    for n in LENGTHS:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0: break
       for shift in SHIFTS:
          # grid
          local_best_score=math.inf; local_best_arr=None; local_best_alpha=None
          for alpha in ALPHAS:
             if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0: break
             arr=make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift)
             arr=normalize_sum(arr,1.0)
             sc=fast_score(arr)
             if np.isfinite(sc): candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
             if sc<local_best_score:
                local_best_score=sc; local_best_arr=arr.copy(); local_best_alpha=float(alpha)
          # golden
          if local_best_alpha is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
             lo=max(0.03, local_best_alpha-0.07); hi=min(0.46, local_best_alpha+0.07)
             if hi-lo > 1e-5:
                opt_alpha=golden_alpha(n, shift, lo, hi, 25)
                arr=normalize_sum(make_sym_power(n, opt_alpha, shift),1.0)
                sc=fast_score(arr)
                if np.isfinite(sc): candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
                if sc<local_best_score: local_best_score=sc; local_best_arr=arr.copy()
          # optional second shift? done
    # Maybe try a few random alpha/shift near best?

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_ALPHA_GRID = (...)
_LENGTH_GRID = (...)
_SHIFT_GRID = (...)

def _normalize_sum(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(max(a.size, 256), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (target / s)
```
If a size 0 returns ones256; but if input empty maybe. Good.

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return math.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return math.inf
    p = a / s
    out = 2*n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < out:
        N <<= 1
    try:
        f = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        spec = f.real*f.real + f.imag*f.imag
        conv = np.fft.irfft(spec, N)
        maxc = float(np.max(conv[:out]))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf
    if not math.isfinite(maxc):
        return math.inf
    if maxc < 0.0:
        maxc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc)
```
If N huge, memory? max 16384? n8192 N16384? out16383 N16384. Actually for n=8192, out=16383, N=16384. Good.

`_make_sym_power`:
```
def _make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift=0.5):
    n=int(n); alpha=float(alpha); shift=float(shift)
    if n <=0: return np.ones(1)
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = i + shift
    right = n - i + (shift - 1.0)
    # For shift 0.5, right up to n-0.5; minimum 0.5
    left = np.maximum(left, 1e-12)
    right = np.maximum(right, 1e-12)
    if abs(alpha) < 1e-14:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.power(left, -alpha) * np.power(right, -alpha)
```
For shift=1, right = n-i, min1. For shift maybe 0.5. Good.

`_alpha_score`:
```
def _alpha_score(alpha, n, shift):
    arr = _make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift)
    arr = _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)
    return _fast_score(arr)
```
`_golden_alpha`:
```
def _golden_alpha(n, shift, lo, hi, max_iter=25):
    ph = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0)/2.0
    c = hi - ph*(hi-lo)
    d = lo + ph*(hi-lo)
    fc = _alpha_score(c,n,shift)
    fd = _alpha_score(d,n,shift)
    for _ in range(max_iter):
       if not (math.isfinite(fc) and math.isfinite(fd)): break
       if fc < fd:
          hi = d; d = c; fd = fc; c = hi - ph*(hi-lo); fc = _alpha_score(c,n,shift)
       else:
          lo = c; c = d; fc = fd; d = lo + ph*(hi-lo); fd = _alpha_score(d,n,shift)
       if hi-lo < 1e-4: break
    return 0.5*(lo+hi)
```
But if fc inf? We can still compare. If both inf, break. Good. However, golden assumes function finite. Grid already finite. ok.

`_exact_score` as above.
`_to_answer_list`:
```
def _to_answer_list(arr):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
       arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64); s=256.0
    arr = arr * (1.0 / s)
    arr = np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```
If arr length 0, use ones256? Add.

`_score_key`: returns item[0].

Main:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
   """..."""
   seed=int(seed)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   reserve=30.0
   deadline=time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - reserve)
   candidates=[]

   # addあれば known parent
   parent = kwargs.get('height_sequence_1', None)
   if parent is None:
      try: parent = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
      except Exception: parent=None
   if parent is not None:
      try:
         p = _normalize_sum(parent,1.0)
         if p.size>0:
            sc = _fast_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc): candidates.append((sc,p.copy()))
      except Exception: pass

   # guaranteed candidates
   guaranteed = ((256,0.0,1.0),(2048,0.25,0.5),(4096,0.25,0.5),(8192,0.25,0.5),(8192,0.25,1.0))
   for n,al,sh in guaranteed:
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0: break
      try:
         arr = _make_sym_power(n, al, sh)
         if n==256 and al==0.0: arr=np.ones(256)
         arr = _normalize_sum(arr,1.0)
         sc=_fast_score(arr)
         if np.isfinite(sc): candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
      except Exception: pass

   # systematic exploration
   if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
      for n in _LENGTH_GRID:
         if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0: break
         for shift in _SHIFT_GRID:
            local_best_score=math.inf
            local_best_alpha=None
            for alpha in _ALPHA_GRID:
               if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0: break
               try:
                  arr=_make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift)
                  arr=_normalize_sum(arr,1.0)
                  sc=_fast_score(arr)
               except Exception:
                  sc=math.inf; arr=None
               if np.isfinite(sc):
                  candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
                  if sc < local_best_score:
                     local_best_score=sc; local_best_alpha=float(alpha)
            if local_best_alpha is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
               lo=max(0.03, local_best_alpha - 0.08)
               hi=min(0.46, local_best_alpha + 0.08)
               if hi - lo > 1e-5:
                  try:
                     opt_alpha=_golden_alpha(n, shift, lo, hi, 25)
                     arr=_make_sym_power(n, opt_alpha, shift)
                     arr=_normalize_sum(arr,1.0)
                     sc=_fast_score(arr)
                     if np.isfinite(sc):
                        candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
                        if sc < local_best_score:
                           local_best_score=sc
                  except Exception:
                     pass
         print(f"finished n={n}") # no lambda; f-string okay
   print("candidates:", len(candidates))
   if candidates:
      candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
   # exact evaluate top
   best_ts=None; best_exact=math.inf; evaluated=0
   for sc, arr in candidates:
      if evaluated >= 6: break
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0: break
      if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
      try:
         lst=_to_answer_list(arr)
         ev=_exact_score(lst)
         evaluated += 1
         if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_exact:
            best_exact=ev; best_ts=lst
      except Exception:
         pass
   if best_ts is None:
      try:
         if candidates:
            best_ts=_to_answer_list(candidates[0][1])
         else:
            best_ts=[1.0]*256
      except Exception:
         best_ts=[1.0]*256
   # maybe final exact sanity? Could call _exact_score(best_ts) but already if evaluated. Print.
   try:
      fs=_exact_score(best_ts)
      print("final exact score:", fs)
   except Exception:
      pass
   return best_ts
```

```python
"""
Minimizer for

    2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2

over non-negative sequences.

The main idea is to use power-law "U-shaped" sequences

    a[i] ~ (i + s)^(-alpha) * (n - i + s)^(-alpha)

which in the continuous limit give a very flat self-convolution and therefore
scores well below the naive all-ones value of 2.  The code searches a small set
of lengths and shifts, uses fast FFT-based objective evaluations for the search,
refines alpha by a golden-section search, and finally checks the best candidates
with the exact evaluator before returning the best valid sequence.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

_ALPHA_GRID = (
    0.0, 0.03, 0.06, 0.09, 0.12, 0.15, 0.18, 0.20, 0.215, 0.23, 0.24,
    0.25, 0.26, 0.275, 0.29, 0.31, 0.34, 0.38, 0.42, 0.45
)

_SHIFT_GRID = (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)

_LENGTH_GRID = (1024, 2048, 4096, 8192)


def _normalize_sum(a, target=1.0):
    """Return a copy of a, nonnegative, finite, and rescaled to sum target."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(max(a.size, 256), dtype=np.float64)

    return a * (float(target) / s)


def _fast_score(a):
    """
    Fast approximation of the true evaluation function using FFT convolution.
    The score is scale invariant, so the input is normalized to sum 1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return math.inf
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return math.inf

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return math.inf

    p = a / s
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    fft_len = 1
    while fft_len < out_len:
        fft_len <<= 1

    try:
        fa = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_len)
        spec = fa.real * fa.real + fa.imag * fa.imag
        conv = np.fft.irfft(spec, fft_len)
        max_c = float(np.max(conv[:out_len]))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(max_c):
        return math.inf
    if max_c < 0.0:
        max_c = 0.0

    return float(2.0 * n * max_c)


def _make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift=0.5):
    """
    Construct the symmetric power-law sequence:

        a[i] = (i + shift)^(-alpha) * (n - i + shift - 1)^(-alpha)

    with alpha >= 0.
    """
    n = int(n)
    alpha = float(alpha)
    shift = float(shift)

    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    left = i + shift
    right = n - i + (shift - 1.0)

    left = np.maximum(left, 1e-12)
    right = np.maximum(right, 1e-12)

    if abs(alpha) < 1e-14:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    return np.power(left, -alpha) * np.power(right, -alpha)


def _alpha_score(alpha, n, shift):
    """Score for one symmetric power-law candidate."""
    arr = _make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift)
    arr = _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)
    return _fast_score(arr)


def _golden_alpha(n, shift, lo, hi, max_iter=25):
    """Golden-section search for the best alpha on [lo, hi]."""
    ph = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    c = hi - ph * (hi - lo)
    d = lo + ph * (hi - lo)

    fc = _alpha_score(c, n, shift)
    fd = _alpha_score(d, n, shift)

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if not (math.isfinite(fc) and math.isfinite(fd)):
            break

        if fc < fd:
            hi = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = hi - ph * (hi - lo)
            fc = _alpha_score(c, n, shift)
        else:
            lo = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = lo + ph * (hi - lo)
            fd = _alpha_score(d, n, shift)

        if hi - lo < 1e-4:
            break

    return 0.5 * (lo + hi)


def _exact_score(sequence):
    """Call the harness evaluator safely."""
    try:
        v = evaluate_sequence(sequence)
        return float(v)
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _to_answer_list(arr):
    """
    Convert an array to a safe final list: nonnegative, finite, sum exactly 1.
    Sum 1 is far above the evaluator's zero-sum guard.
    """
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return [1.0] * 256

    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 256.0

    arr = arr * (1.0 / s)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in arr]


def _score_key(item):
    """Sort key for candidate tuples (score, array)."""
    return item[0]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a low-scoring nonnegative sequence.

    Strategy:
      1. Start from the old parent if it is available, but primarily use
         new symmetric power-law constructions.
      2. Evaluate candidates quickly with FFT convolution.
      3. Refine the exponent alpha by golden-section search.
      4. Verify the best candidates using the exact evaluator and return
         the best exact-valid sequence.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    total_budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    work_budget = max(0.0, total_budget - max(20.0, 0.05 * total_budget))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, work_budget)

    candidates = []

    # Include the previous construction if it exists, parent or global.
    parent = kwargs.get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent is None:
        try:
            parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            p = _normalize_sum(parent, 1.0)
            if p.size > 0:
                sc = _fast_score(p)
                if math.isfinite(sc):
                    candidates.append((sc, p.copy()))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Guaranteed fallback: uniform sequence, which scores exactly 2.
    try:
        uniform = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        candidates.append((_fast_score(uniform), uniform.copy()))
    except Exception:
        candidates.append((2.0, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64).copy()))

    # Guaranteed strong candidates even if the budget is tiny.
    guaranteed = (
        (512, 0.25, 0.5),
        (2048, 0.25, 0.5),
        (4096, 0.25, 0.5),
        (8192, 0.25, 0.5),
        (8192, 0.25, 1.0),
    )

    for n, alpha, shift in guaranteed:
        try:
            arr = _make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift)
            arr = _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)
            sc = _fast_score(arr)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    print(f"guaranteed candidates: {len(candidates)}")

    # Systematic search over length and shift, with grid + golden refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        for n in _LENGTH_GRID:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                break

            for shift in _SHIFT_GRID:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                    break

                local_best_score = math.inf
                local_best_alpha = None

                for alpha in _ALPHA_GRID:
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                        break

                    try:
                        arr = _make_sym_power(n, alpha, shift)
                        arr = _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)
                        sc = _fast_score(arr)
                    except Exception:
                        arr = None
                        sc = math.inf

                    if math.isfinite(sc):
                        candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
                        if sc < local_best_score:
                            local_best_score = sc
                            local_best_alpha = float(alpha)

                # Refine around the best alpha found on this grid.
                if local_best_alpha is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
                    lo = max(0.03, local_best_alpha - 0.08)
                    hi = min(0.46, local_best_alpha + 0.08)
                    if hi - lo > 1e-5:
                        try:
                            opt_alpha = _golden_alpha(n, shift, lo, hi, 25)
                            arr = _make_sym_power(n, opt_alpha, shift)
                            arr = _normalize_sum(arr, 1.0)
                            sc = _fast_score(arr)
                            if math.isfinite(sc):
                                candidates.append((sc, arr.copy()))
                                if sc < local_best_score:
                                    local_best_score = sc
                        except Exception:
                            pass

            print(f"finished length {n}")

    print(f"total FFT-scored candidates: {len(candidates)}")

    if candidates:
        candidates.sort(key=_score_key)

    # Verify the best few candidates exactly.
    best_sequence = None
    best_exact_score = math.inf
    exact_attempts = 0

    if candidates:
        for fast_sc, arr in candidates:
            if exact_attempts >= 6:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            if not math.isfinite(fast_sc):
                continue

            try:
                seq = _to_answer_list(arr)
                ev = _exact_score(seq)
                exact_attempts += 1
                if math.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_exact_score:
                    best_exact_score = ev
                    best_sequence = seq
            except Exception:
                pass

    # If exact verification failed, fall back to the best FFT-scored candidate.
    if best_sequence is None:
        if candidates:
            try:
                best_sequence = _to_answer_list(candidates[0][1])
            except Exception:
                best_sequence = [1.0] * 256
        else:
            best_sequence = [1.0] * 256

    # Final log.  If exact score is already known, avoid an extra heavy call.
    if math.isfinite(best_exact_score):
        print(f"final exact score: {best_exact_score:.12f}")
    else:
        try:
            final_exact = _exact_score(best_sequence)
            if math.isfinite(final_exact):
                best_exact_score = final_exact
            print(f"final exact score: {best_exact_score if math.isfinite(best_exact_score) else final_exact}")
        except Exception:
            print("final exact score: unknown")

    return best_sequence
```
